Trai","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-12T16:58:21Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2607_10836.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是多智能体 LLM 系统里的一个老问题：如果用一组较小、异构、专长不同的模型协同推理，怎样才能让系统按题目难度自适应地决定‘找谁来做、做到多深、谁和谁需要通信’，而不是像固定 ensemble 那样每次都把所有 agent 全跑一遍。作者还额外处理了一个很实际的问题：当某个 expert 在部署时被替换后，原先学到的 gate 阈值会失效，系统需要一种低成本的重新校准机制。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接相关性有限，它更接近 post-train 阶段的多智能体推理系统与 RL 协调方法，而不是预训练方法本身。启发点主要有两类。第一，它把 MoE/conditional compute 的思想搬到了 agent 层：query-dependent routing、adaptive depth、selective communication，这些设计和大模型里 token-level 或 expert-level 路由有相通之处。第二，它展示了在冻结专家、只训练轻量协调层的设定下，RL 可以学到比手工协议更细的协作策略，这对研究 pretrain 与 post-train 接口、以及如何把计算预算分配给不同推理路径，有一定参考价值。但如果只从预训练研究优先级看，这篇不属于必须精读的那类。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者提出 GRADE，一个分层多智能体框架。系统分三层：最上层是一个小型 orchestrator M0，负责接收共享 encoder 产出的 query 表示并做初步决策；中间层是两个 manager，用 MLP 形成分组信号，把相关 specialist 组织到各自子池中；底层是若干 sub-agent，每个 agent 绑定一个冻结的 expert LLM，同时带有少量可训练模块，包括把 expert 输出映射到共享 d 维空间的 adapter、answer head，以及用于热替换后的 calibration map。\n\n核心机制是四个 gate。Assignment Gate 决定激活哪些 sub-agent；Depth Gate 决定是否继续下探到更深层级或提前停止；Communication Gate 决定 agent 间哪些消息需要传递，避免全广播；Pruning Gate 则在分支展开后裁掉低价值路径。这样做的目标很明确：把多智能体系统从固定拓扑、固定通信、固定深度，改成按 query 自适应分配计算。\n\n训练上，作者提出 CoGRPO，可以看成 group-relative policy optimization 在多智能体层级系统里的改写。它不需要 critic，而是基于 rollout 的相对奖励给所有参与当前轨迹的 gate 和 agent 分配 advantage。这里的关键不是 RL 名字本身，而是 credit assignment：因为最终只有一个 correctness reward，系统必须把这个稀疏信号回传给路由决策、通信决策和具体回答 agent。作者的做法是把所有对当前 rollout 有贡献的模块都纳入同一个相对优势更新框架。\n\n另一个比较实用的设计是 Expert Registry + calibration。每个 agent 不直接和某个固定 expert 强绑定，而是通过 registry descriptor 与可替换 expert 关联。部署时如果替换 expert，只需用 64 个 anchor queries 对该 agent 的 calibration map 做重新校准，而不必重训 gates。作者强调这是因为不同 expert 的输出分布会漂移，若不校准，原有 gate 阈值和通信决策都会偏掉。","experiments":"实验主要回答五件事：是否比 monolithic 或 flat ensemble 更省算力；四种 gate 各自贡献多大；CoGRPO 的 credit assignment 是否优于替代方案；expert hot-swap 后校准是否必要；以及难题是否真的会触发更多 agent 和更深层级。\n\n结果上，GRADE 在平均约 17B active parameters 下，GSM8K 达到 94.8±0.3%，MMLUPro 78.2±0.6%，GPQA 57.4±1.8%，相对 Puppeteer 在 MMLUPro 提升 4.8 个点、在 GPQA 提升 3.3 个点。AIME-2025 上，Puppeteer 的 4-model brute-force ensemble 仍高 1.9 个点，说明在高强度数学推理上，单纯增加并行大模型推理深度仍有优势。\n\n消融方面，论文的主线结论是：层级路由、选择性通信、深度自适应不是可互相替代的小组件，去掉任一部分都会带来明显退化；而 CoGRPO 比若干简单 credit assignment 方案更稳，因为它能把最终正确性奖励更合理地分给参与轨迹的 gate 与 agent。关于部署鲁棒性，去掉 calibration 后，expert 热替换会立刻造成约 18 个点的准确率下降，且需要 20+ 查询才能自然恢复；加入 calibration 后，恢复只需 0–9 个查询。这个结果虽然偏系统工程，但很实用。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看方法总览图和四个 gate 的定义，弄清楚系统到底在哪些位置做条件计算。然后重点看 CoGRPO 的 credit assignment 设计，因为这是区别于手工 orchestration 的核心。实验部分优先看三类表：一是和 Puppeteer、flat ensemble、monolithic baseline 的主结果；二是 gate/communication/depth 的消融；三是 expert substitution 与 calibration 的恢复曲线。\n\n如果你的兴趣点是预训练，只需要花 25–35 分钟抓住 conditional compute 和 routing 设计即可，不必深挖所有 benchmark。若你做 post-train、agent RL 或 inference-time orchestration，这篇可以再细看训练细节和热替换实验。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a practical We show this net has a fundamental hole. A distributed backdoor splits a harmful payload across agents, so every local check passes while the assembled object is the attack. The monitor can be right on every step and still miss the attack. The problem is not splitting itself: split fragments can still leak suspicious tokens or provenance edges. An attacker can split a harmful payload across multiple agent fragments such that every fragment passes local inspection, yet the assembled object is malicious. The paper investigates the conditions under which a local monitor still retains evidence of the attack, and when that evidence has fully left its observational scope.","importance":"This work has direct implications for LLM safety and alignment research. Current dominant runtime defenses—prompt injection detection, per-message moderation, taint tracking—all implicitly assume attack signals reside within a single step. The paper demonstrates both theoretically and experimentally a fundamental limit: when an attack is decomposed into locally benign fragments, no amount of local classifier strength can detect it. This shifts the design of agent safety architectures toward compositional monitoring rather than purely local checks.","technical_approach":"The paper formalizes the concept of an observability boundary: a monitor can only detect what its observation window can still distinguish from benign traffic. The observation window ranges from a single message to multiple messages to the full assembled result. Two key conditions are defined: distributed backdoor (the attack payload is split across K fragments and only causes harm after composition) and local benignness (each fragment appears benign in the monitor's local view). Proposition 1 proves that once fragments are locally benign, no detector based on that local view can cross the observability boundary. The central technical artifact is a testbed with a single dial: the final assembled attack is held fixed, and only the amount of visible attack residue in a single fragment is varied, isolating the true reason a monitor passes or fails.","experiments":"Experiments span three settings: a custom testbed, an external prompt-injection benchmark with a trained detector, and full agent runs. Multiple monitor types are compared: per-step monitors, trained prompt-injection detectors, taint checks following visible data paths, and marker-based / marker-free assembly monitors. The core finding is consistent across settings: splitting the attack does not defeat a local monitor—removing usable evidence does. Once every fragment looks benign and revealing cues are gone from the monitor's view, none of the tested monitors catch the attack. Detection signal returns only when the monitor observes the reassembled attack. The paper also shows that a wider view alone is insufficient: full-trace monitors, decoders, and trained detectors still miss the attack unless they recover the representation that exposes the payload.","reading_guide":"Skim-level reading, approximately 20-30 minutes. Focus on Section 2 for the formalization and the proof of Proposition 1—grasp the two core concepts of observability boundary and local benignness. Figure 1 in the Introduction provides a clear intuitive illustration. In the experiments, pay attention to the comparison across monitor types on the same set of fragments, which shows why stronger local classifiers cannot solve the problem. This recipe is simple and low-cost, but it only learns to imitate the sequence of expert actions, rather than training the agent to choose the right action against plausible mistakes at each state. Existing methods to mitigate this problem include preference learning or reinforcement learning, but they usually need ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-12T06:38:55Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2607_10601.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Agentic-DPO 解决的是 agent 轨迹训练中的行为克隆错配问题：SFT 只学习专家轨迹的 token 序列，却没有把每一步工具调用、动作选择和参数生成当作状态条件下的决策来优化。","importance":"这项工作对 LLM agent 的 post-train 有直接参考价值，也对预训练研究中的“轨迹数据如何转成更强训练信号”有启发。关键贡献不是提出新的在线 RL，而是证明专家轨迹本身可以被重写成局部偏好数据：在同一个状态下，专家动作作为正例，当前学生模型的一步采样动作作为负例。这样能让模型学习“在当前状态不要选自己容易犯的错”，比单纯模仿专家 token 更接近策略学习。它的价值在于成本结构清楚：不需要环境 rollout、不需要 reward model、不需要完整轨迹探索，适合已有大量专家轨迹但在线交互昂贵的场景。","technical_approach":"方法分三层。第一层是把专家轨迹拆成状态-动作决策点。对每个专家动作状态，模型先看到当前状态，再从学生策略采样一个一步动作作为负样本，专家动作作为偏好正样本，然后用 DPO 风格目标拉开二者的相对概率。这里的关键是 rollout 只到当前动作，不执行环境，因此负样本反映当前学生策略的局部错误，而不是完整失败轨迹。第二层是 SFT anchor。作者没有直接用偏好学习从零学动作格式，而是保留 SFT 约束，让模型先维持合法的工具调用和输出格式，避免 DPO 在结构化 action string 上训练不稳。第三层是 Policy-Preserving Augmentation，简称 PPA。agent 动作文本通常同时包含“策略决策”和“schema 渲染方式”，DPO 可能把 schema 表面差异也当作偏好方向。PPA 将同一条潜在轨迹渲染到多个 action schema 下，保持潜在策略不变，只改变表面形式，使偏好梯度更集中在动作选择本身，而不是具体格式。","experiments":"实验覆盖 StableToolBench、τ-bench retail 和 Mind2Web，分别对应受控工具调用、长程用户交互和网页 GUI grounding。给出的主要结果较明确：在 StableToolBench 上，Qwen3.5-2B 经 SFT 的 canonical accuracy 为 57.1%，Agentic-DPO 提升到 90.9%。在 τ-bench retail 上，Qwen3.5-9B 的成功率达到 41.4%，高于同 backbone 的 GRPO 40.0%，但不需要在线 RL rollout。在 Mind2Web 上，跨 task、跨 website、跨 domain 三类 held-out split 的平均 step success 为 64.4%。这些结果支持一个结论：对于小模型 agent，专家轨迹中的状态结构没有被 SFT 充分利用；用当前学生的一步错误构造局部偏好，能显著提高动作选择质量。需要注意的是，摘要和正文片段主要展示 benchmark 结果，若精读应进一步检查负样本采样温度、DPO beta、SFT/DPO 配比、PPA 视图数量，以及不同 benchmark 上 gains 是否来自格式修正还是真实决策改进。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25–35 分钟。先看 Introduction 中 Figure 1，确认它和 SFT、Step-DPO、PivotRL、Agent-RLVR 的成本差异。再看 Method 的 Algorithm 1、DPO loss 定义、SFT anchor 和 PPA 设计，重点判断负样本是否足够“像模型会犯的错”。实验部分优先看 StableToolBench 的大幅提升是否有 action-format 相关因素，再看 τ-bench retail 与 GRPO 的同 backbone 对比，最后看 Mind2Web 的分 split 结果。消融部分若完整正文提供，应重点看去掉 SFT anchor、去掉 PPA、改变负样本来源后的退化幅度。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Agentic-DPO addresses a mismatch in agent trajectory training: SFT treats expert trajectories as token sequences, while an agent actually needs to learn state-conditioned decisions over tool calls, actions, arguments, and final responses.","importance":"The work is most relevant to agent post-training, but it also carries a useful lesson for pretraining researchers who care about converting trajectories into stronger learning signals. Its main contribution is not online RL. It shows that expert trajectories can be transformed into local preference supervision: at each expert state, the expert action is preferred over a one-step action sampled from the current student policy. This gives the model direct contrastive feedback against its own likely mistakes. The cost profile is the main appeal: no environment rollout, no reward model, and no full-trajectory exploration.","technical_approach":"The method first decomposes expert trajectories into state-action decision points. For each expert action state, the current student samples a one-step action without executing it in the environment. The expert action and this student action form a preference pair, trained with a DPO-style objective. This differs from standard behavior cloning because the model is not only asked to raise the probability of the demonstrated action; it is also asked to lower the relative probability of a plausible wrong action under the same state. Two stabilizers are important. The SFT anchor keeps the model close to valid expert behavior and action formatting, so preference learning does not have to discover structured tool syntax by itself. Policy-Preserving Augmentation renders the same latent trajectory under multiple schemas while preserving the underlying policy. This reduces gradients that are dominated by schema-level surface differences rather than actual decision quality.","experiments":"The evaluation uses StableToolBench, τ-bench retail, and Mind2Web, covering controlled tool use, long-horizon user-interleaved interaction, and web GUI grounding. On StableToolBench, Qwen3.5-2B improves from 57.1% SFT canonical accuracy to 90.9% with Agentic-DPO. On τ-bench retail, Qwen3.5-9B reaches 41.4% success, compared with 40.0% for GRPO under the same backbone, while avoiding online RL rollouts. On Mind2Web, the method reports 64.4% average step success across cross-task, cross-website, and cross-domain held-out splits. These numbers suggest that SFT leaves substantial decision-level signal unused in expert trajectories, especially for smaller agents. A careful reading should still check whether the gains come from better action selection, better schema handling, or both.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is enough for most readers. Start with Figure 1 and the introduction to understand the cost difference from SFT, Step-DPO, PivotRL, and Agent-RLVR. In the experiments, focus on the StableToolBench jump, the same-backbone comparison with GRPO on τ-bench retail, and the split-level Mind2Web results. memory-augmented agent 如何消费检索到的记忆，尤其是在记忆与当前任务冲突时，模型是在何处采纳错误信息、错误如何沿轨迹传播，以及是否能自我修正。","importance":"它不是基座预训练方法论文，但对长程 agent 的训练与评测有参考意义。现有 memory agent 研究常把问题放在“写什么、存哪里、检索哪条”上，默认模型会合理使用检索内容。这个假设在长程交互中很脆弱：错误记忆一旦在早期动作中被采纳，后续观察和动作会进入另一条状态分支，简单的最终成功率很难解释失败来自检索、采纳、传播还是恢复能力。E-P-R 把 memory consumption 拆成 Entry、Propagation、Recovery 三个阶段，为训练控制策略、设计拒绝错误记忆的数据、构造 agent 评测协议提供了更细的诊断维度。","technical_approach":"核心方法是固定 memory 内容，改变 memory 的可见时机，从而把检索质量和模型消费行为分开。memory 内容分为三类：helpful memory 提供任务相关指导，conflicting memory 提供看似合理但任务错误的指导，same-website cross-task control memory 作为内容控制。注入 schedule 分为 early、persistent 和 late：early 只在第一个可影响决策点出现，persistent 在每一步都出现，late 在若干动作之后才出现。E-P-R 三个指标分别对应不同机制：Entry 看记忆是否改变首次暴露后的动作；Propagation 看初始改变在后续步骤是否继续影响轨迹，尤其是在重复暴露下是否被放大；Recovery 看模型偏离后能否回到任务正确路径。这个设计的优点是变量干净：它不把 memory 系统的 retrieval 误差和 LLM policy 的服从倾向混在一起。","experiments":"实验使用两个测试环境。第一个是在 WebArena 上做 paired protocol，保持 memory passage 不变，只改变注入阶段，用 early、persistent、late schedule 粗粒度探测 E-P-R。模型覆盖 Qwen3.5、Gemma4、Gemini-3 三个系列的五个模型。观察到的模式较一致：错误记忆容易在早期进入决策，重复暴露会放大影响，损害主要集中在采纳冲突记忆的轨迹上；一旦轨迹因为错误 memory 分叉，后续恢复困难。作者将这一现象称为 compliance trap。第二个是 MemTrapBench，用更受控的 trap 结构分别测量 uptake、persistence 和 correction，用于验证“首次采纳冲突记忆是最终失败主因”这一机制假设。摘要和正文片段没有给出完整数值表，因此精读时需要重点核对各模型的 entry rate、propagation rate、recovery rate，以及强模型是否只是平均成功率更高但被冲突记忆造成的绝对损害更大。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20–30 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 supply-side memory 和 consumption-side memory 的区分，这决定了它和 Reflexion、ExpeL、AWM、MemGPT 等工作的差异。再看 Method 里 memory 类型、注入 schedule、E-P-R 指标定义。实验部分优先看 WebArena 的 paired protocol，而不是只看最终成功率；随后看 MemTrapBench 如何把 uptake、persistence、correction 分开。对做 agent 训练的人，更值得关注的是 conflicting memory 样本能否转成拒绝错误上下文的 SFT/DPO/RL 数据，以及 persistent exposure 为什么会让模型更难恢复。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"This work studies how memory-augmented agents consume retrieved memories over a trajectory, especially when the memory conflicts with the current task: where the wrong memory first changes behavior, how that change propagates, and whether the agent can recover.","importance":"The paper is not about base-model pretraining, but it is useful for agent training and evaluation. Much of the memory-agent literature focuses on the supply side: what to write, how to store it, and which memory to retrieve. This work shifts attention to the policy that consumes the retrieved text. That distinction matters because a plausible memory can still be harmful if the model follows it at the wrong time or repeats it after the state has changed. The Entry–Propagation–Recovery decomposition gives a more informative diagnostic than final success rate. It can guide training data construction for memory rejection, control policies for memory exposure, and benchmarks for long-horizon agent reliability.","technical_approach":"The method fixes the memory content and intervenes on when the agent can see it. Memory passages are grouped into helpful memories, conflicting memories, and same-website cross-task control memories. Exposure schedules include early injection, persistent injection, and late injection. Entry measures whether the memory changes the first action after exposure. Propagation measures whether the initial change continues to shape later actions, especially under repeated exposure. Recovery measures whether the agent can return to a task-correct trajectory after memory-induced divergence. This design separates retrieval quality from consumption behavior: the retrieved text is held fixed, so failures can be attributed to how the policy uses or overuses the memory.","experiments":"The evaluation uses WebArena and a controlled benchmark called MemTrapBench. On WebArena, the authors run a paired protocol where the same memory passage is injected under different schedules. The tested models span five systems from the Qwen3.5, Gemma4, and Gemini-3 families. The reported pattern is consistent: conflicting memories enter early, repeated exposure amplifies their effect, and the damage is concentrated on trajectories that comply with the conflicting memory. Once the trajectory branches because of a wrong memory, recovery is difficult. MemTrapBench is used to validate the same mechanism in a more controlled trap structure by separately measuring uptake, persistence, and correction. The provided text does not include full numeric tables, so the main items to verify in a full read are entry rate, propagation rate, recovery rate, and how these vary with model strength.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim should cover the main value. In the experiments, prioritize the paired WebArena protocol over aggregate success rate, then check how MemTrapBench separates uptake, persistence, and correction.
